Codename Droid disassembly
==========================
Codename Droid was written by Nicholas Chamberlain and Martin Edmondson, and
was published by Superior Software for the BBC Micro in 1987. It is an arcade
adventure game in which the player must seize a spaceship from an underground
base. It is the sequel to Superior's 1986 game, Stryker's Run.

The following disassembly was created by reverse engineering binary images,
without access to any source code. It is nevertheless reasonably complete,
allowing the technical approaches used to be understood.

The author of this disassembly imposes no additional copyright restrictions
beyond those already present on the game itself. It is provided for educational
purposes only, and it is hoped that the original authors will accept it in the
good faith it was intended - as a tribute to their skills.

Interesting pokes
=================
&23a3 = &a9, &2e3a = &a9, &2f11 = &a9, &2f13 = &a9
infinite energy

&3d88 = &a9
infinite blaster

&16e4 = &a9
infinite passcards

&3013 = &a2
infinite energy cells

&1271 = &a9
infinite jet pack fuel

&2a73 = &a9
infinite lives

Game disassembly
================
; DROID
; 00FF1100 00FF5800 4800

; new_game
&1100 20 54 27 JSR &2754 ; to_remove_lifts_from_level_data
&1103 20 1e 27 JSR &271e ; to_title_screen
&1106 20 74 12 JSR &1274 ; set_level_specific_tiles
; main_game_loop
&1109 ad 62 fe LDA &fe62 ; User VIA data direction register B
&110c c9 d4    CMP #&d4
&110e f0 03    BEQ &1113 ; skip_reset
&1110 6c fc ff JMP (&fffc) ; os_reset_address_low
; skip_reset
&1113 20 19 27 JSR &2719 ; wait_for_vsync
&1116 20 7b 27 JSR &277b ; to_update_exploding_mines
&1119 a2 c8    LDX #&c8 
&111b 86 5f    STX &5f ; enemy_energy_and_initial_flags             # Set to >= &20 to suppress clearing collisions
&111d 86 7e    STX &7e ; enemy_to_update                            # Set to negative to indicate collision with player
&111f 20 5d 27 JSR &275d ; to_buffer_colliding_blasters
&1122 20 1a 12 JSR &121a ; update_player
&1125 20 60 27 JSR &2760 ; to_check_for_collision_with_blasters     # Check for collision with player and walls
&1128 a5 6f    LDA &6f ; suit_energy
&112a 10 03    BPL &112f ; not_out_of_energy
&112c 20 63 27 JSR &2763 ; to_lose_life
; not_out_of_energy
&112f 20 6f 27 JSR &276f ; to_remove_colliding_blasters
&1132 20 b1 18 JSR &18b1 ; check_for_player_input_other_than_movement
&1135 a9 01    LDA #&01
&1137 85 e8    STA &e8 ; suppress_horizontal_falling                # Set to non-zero to fall vertically
&1139 20 f5 21 JSR &21f5 ; consider_player_falling
&113c a9 00    LDA #&00
&113e 85 e8    STA &e8 ; suppress_horizontal_falling                # Set to non-zero to move horizontally when falling
&1140 20 cb 11 JSR &11cb ; reset_autorepeat_suppression_for_fire_down_and_up
&1143 20 19 27 JSR &2719 ; wait_for_vsync
&1146 20 2d 27 JSR &272d ; to_update_blasters
&1149 20 e5 11 JSR &11e5 ; set_colour_zero_to_black
&114c 20 19 27 JSR &2719 ; wait_for_vsync
&114f e6 5e    INC &5e ; frame_counter
&1151 20 f5 21 JSR &21f5 ; consider_player_falling
&1154 a9 00    LDA #&00
&1156 85 6e    STA &6e ; screen_was_scrolled                        # Set to zero to indicate screen wasn't scrolled
&1158 20 e2 1e JSR &1ee2 ; update_horizontally_jumping_player
&115b 20 31 1f JSR &1f31 ; consider_jumping_onto_rope
&115e 20 cb 11 JSR &11cb ; check_for_player_stance_and_firing_keys
&1161 a5 c2    LDA &c2 ; objects_carried + &2 (OBJECT_JET_PACK)     # Non-zero if player is in jet pack
&1163 f0 04    BEQ &1169 ; skip_suppressing_jump
&1165 a9 00    LDA #&00
&1167 85 95    STA &95 ; jump_direction                             # Set to zero to indicate player not jumping horizontally
; skip_suppressing_jump
&1169 a5 6c    LDA &6c ; player_swapping_sides_on_rope              # Non-zero if player swapping sides on rope
&116b f0 18    BEQ &1185 ; not_changing_direction_on_rope
&116d a5 94    LDA &94 ; player_direction                           # Zero if facing right, non-zero if facing left
&116f f0 0a    BEQ &117b ; is_facing_right
; is_facing_left
&1171 e6 93    INC &93 ; player_x
&1173 20 3c 13 JSR &133c ; consider_scrolling_right
&1176 c6 6c    DEC &6c ; player_swapping_sides_on_rope              # Set to zero to indicate side swapped
&1178 4c 88 11 JMP &1188 ; skip_checking_for_player_input
; is_facing_right
&117b c6 93    DEC &93 ; player_x
&117d 20 65 14 JSR &1465 ; consider_scrolling_left
&1180 c6 6c    DEC &6c ; player_swapping_sides_on_rope              # Set to zero to indicate side swapped
&1182 4c 88 11 JMP &1188 ; skip_checking_for_player_input
; not_changing_direction_on_rope
&1185 20 7f 17 JSR &177f ; consider_checking_for_player_input
; skip_checking_for_player_input
&1188 a5 6e    LDA &6e ; screen_was_scrolled                        # Non-zero if screen was scrolled
&118a d0 03    BNE &118f ; skip_waiting_for_vsync
&118c 20 19 27 JSR &2719 ; wait_for_vsync
; skip_waiting_for_vsync
&118f 20 2d 27 JSR &272d ; to_update_blasters
&1192 20 e5 11 JSR &11e5 ; set_colour_zero_to_black
&1195 20 7e 27 JSR &277e ; update_enemies
&1198 20 45 27 JSR &2745 ; to_update_events
&119b a5 5e    LDA &5e ; frame_counter
&119d 29 0f    AND #&0f
&119f c9 07    CMP #&07
&11a1 90 08    BCC &11ab ; unflip_chandelier
; flip_chandelier
&11a3 a9 02    LDA #&02 ; FLIP_HORIZONTALLY
&11a5 8d da 0f STA &0fda ; tile_sprites_addresses_table + 2 * &2d (TILE_CHANDELIER)
&11a8 4c b0 11 JMP &11b0 ; skip_unflipping_chandelier
; unflip_chandelier
&11ab a9 00    LDA #&00
&11ad 8d da 0f STA &0fda ; tile_sprites_addresses_table + 2 * &2d (TILE_CHANDELIER)
; skip_unflipping_chandelier
&11b0 a5 5e    LDA &5e ; frame_counter
&11b2 29 06    AND #&06
&11b4 c9 04    CMP #&04
&11b6 d0 10    BNE &11c8 ; skip_updating_ancient_shrine
&11b8 a5 9f    LDA &9f ; level
&11ba c9 03    CMP #&03
&11bc 90 0a    BCC &11c8 ; skip_updating_ancient_shrine
&11be c9 06    CMP #&06
&11c0 b0 06    BCS &11c8 ; skip_updating_ancient_shrine
&11c2 20 48 27 JSR &2748 ; to_update_chandeliers
&11c5 20 36 27 JSR &2736 ; to_update_gargoyles
; skip_updating_ancient_shrine
&11c8 4c 09 11 JMP &1109 ; main_game_loop

; reset_autorepeat_suppression_for_fire_down_and_up
&11cb a2 00    LDX #&00
; reset_autorepeat_suppression_for_fire_down_and_up_loop
&11cd bd e2 11 LDA &11e2,X ; stance_and_firing_keys
&11d0 c5 ec    CMP &ec ; os_most_recently_pressed_key
&11d2 f0 08    BEQ &11dc ; consider_next_key
&11d4 c5 ed    CMP &ed ; os_first_pressed_key
&11d6 f0 04    BEQ &11dc ; consider_next_key
&11d8 a9 00    LDA #&00
&11da 95 68    STA &68,X ; player_recent_keys
; consider_next_key
&11dc e8       INX
&11dd e0 03    CPX #&03
&11df d0 ec    BNE &11cd ; reset_autorepeat_suppression_for_fire_down_and_up_loop
&11e1 60       RTS

; stance_and_firing_keys
&11e2 d8 ; ]
&11e3 e8 ; /
&11e4 c8 ; '

; set_colour_zero_to_black
&11e5 a2 00    LDX #&00
&11e7 20 42 27 JSR &2742 ; to_set_palette
&11ea a2 10    LDX #&10
&11ec 20 42 27 JSR &2742 ; to_set_palette
&11ef a2 40    LDX #&40
&11f1 20 42 27 JSR &2742 ; to_set_palette
&11f4 a2 50    LDX #&50
&11f6 4c 42 27 JMP &2742 ; to_set_palette

; consider_plotting_star
&11f9 a8       TAY
&11fa 29 01    AND #&01
&11fc d0 01    BNE &11ff ; is_top_two_tiles
&11fe 60       RTS
; is_top_two_tiles
&11ff b9 00 ee LDA &ee00,Y                                          # Use OS ROM as source of random positions for stars
&1202 4a       LSR A
&1203 4a       LSR A
&1204 a8       TAY
&1205 20 21 27 JSR &2721 ; to_calculate_screen_address
&1208 a0 00    LDY #&00
&120a b1 70    LDA (&70),Y ; screen_address
&120c d0 04    BNE &1212 ; leave
&120e a9 88    LDA #&88 ; 3000
&1210 91 70    STA (&70),Y ; screen_address                         # Plot star
&1212 60       RTS

; leave_if_player_is_jumping_or_falling
&1213 a5 8f    LDA &8f ; player_jumping_or_falling
&1215 f0 02    BEQ &1219 ; leave
&1217 68       PLA
&1218 68       PLA
; leave
&1219 60       RTS

; update_player
&121a a5 6d    LDA &6d ; player_stance
&121c d0 05    BNE &1223 ; not_standing
&121e c6 92    DEC &92 ; player_frame
&1220 4c a2 17 JMP &17a2 ; plot_player_facing_direction
; not_standing
&1223 c9 01    CMP #&01 ; STANCE_KNEELING
&1225 d0 03    BNE &122a ; not_kneeling
&1227 4c b1 1f JMP &1fb1 ; plot_kneeling_player
; not_kneeling
&122a c9 02    CMP #&02 ; STANCE_CRAWLING
&122c d0 0d    BNE &123b ; not_crawling
&122e 20 e1 12 JSR &12e1 ; get_player_screen_address
&1231 a5 94    LDA &94 ; player_direction                           # Zero if facing right, non-zero if facing left
&1233 d0 03    BNE &1238 ; to_plot_player_crawling_left
&1235 4c 61 21 JMP &2161 ; plot_player_crawling_right               # &2161 and &21af are functionally identical
; to_plot_player_crawling_left
&1238 4c af 21 JMP &21af ; plot_player_crawling_left
; not_crawling
&123b c9 03    CMP #&03 ; STANCE_CLIMBING
&123d d0 03    BNE &1242 ; not_climbing
&123f 4c 2f 26 JMP &262f ; plot_climbing_player
; not_climbing
&1242 c9 f0    CMP #&f0 ; STANCE_DEAD
&1244 f0 0f    BEQ &1255 ; leave
&1246 a2 03    LDX #&03
&1248 a0 20    LDY #&20
&124a 20 e1 12 JSR &12e1 ; get_player_screen_address
&124d 4c 1b 1e JMP &1e1b ; plot_player_in_jet_pack

; consider_player_falling_and_leave_parent
&1250 20 f5 21 JSR &21f5 ; consider_player_falling
&1253 68       PLA                                                  # Leave consider_jumping_onto_rope on next return
&1254 68       PLA
; leave
&1255 60       RTS

; update_jet_pack

&1258 d0 0b    BNE &1265 ; not_out_of_energy
; exit_jet_pack
&125a a9 ff    LDA #&ff
&125c 85 e2    STA &e2 ; jet_pack_energy
&125e a9 00    LDA #&00
&1260 85 c2    STA &c2 ; objects_carried + &2 (OBJECT_JET_PACK)
&1262 4c 9e 17 JMP &179e ; plot_player_standing
; not_out_of_energy
&1265 a5 c2    LDA &c2 ; objects_carried + &2 (OBJECT_JET_PACK)     # Non-zero if player is in jet pack
&1267 f0 0a    BEQ &1273 ; leave
&1269 e6 f8    INC &f8 ; jet_pack_energy_cooldown
&126b a5 f8    LDA &f8 ; jet_pack_energy_cooldown

&126f d0 02    BNE &1273 ; leave
&1271 c6 e2    DEC &e2 ; jet_pack_energy

&1273 60       RTS

&127c 8e 46 05 STX &0546 ; level_tiles_translation_table + &20 + &6 # Use tables for Crew's Quarters sectors one and two
&127f ca       DEX ; &1c : TILE_TABLE_LEFT
&1280 8e 47 05 STX &0547 ; level_tiles_translation_table + &20 + &7
&1283 60       RTS
; use_pipes
&1284 a9 06    LDA #&06 ; TILE_PIPE_BOTTOM_RIGHT
&1286 8d 46 05 STA &0546 ; level_tiles_translation_table + &20 + &6 # Use pipes for Crew's Quarters sector three
&1289 a9 04    LDA #&04 ; TILE_PIPE_BOTTOM_LEFT
&128b 8d 47 05 STA &0547 ; level_tiles_translation_table + &20 + &7
&128e a5 9f    LDA &9f ; level
&1290 c9 0a    CMP #&0a                                             # Is this Missile Factory sectors two or three?
&1292 b0 0b    BCS &129f ; use_spaceships
; use_missiles
&1294 a9 30    LDA #&30 ; TILE_MISSILE_TAIL_LEFT

&1299 a9 2e    LDA #&2e ; TILE_MISSILE_NOSE_LEFT

&129e 60       RTS
; use_spaceships

&12a9 60       RTS

; update_tune

&3598 c9 ff    CMP #&ff

&359c a9 01    LDA #&01

&7b02 20 ee ff JSR &ffee ; OSWRCH
&7b05 a9 02    LDA #&02
&7b07 20 ee ff JSR &ffee ; OSWRCH
&7b0a a9 06    LDA #&06 ; C
&7b0c 20 ee ff JSR &ffee ; OSWRCH
&7b0f a9 00    LDA #&00
&7b11 20 ee ff JSR &ffee ; OSWRCH
&7b14 20 ee ff JSR &ffee ; OSWRCH
&7b17 20 ee ff JSR &ffee ; OSWRCH
&7b1a a9 05    LDA #&05                                             # Set addressable latch B5 to 0
&7b1c 8d 40 fe STA &fe40 ; System VIA port B input/output register
&7b1f a9 0c    LDA #&0c                                             # Set addressable latch B4 to 1, i.e. screen scrolls from &6000
&7b21 8d 40 fe STA &fe40 ; System VIA port B input/output register
&7b24 a2 00    LDX #&00
; write_video_registers_loop
&7b26 bd f4 7b LDA &7bf4,X ; video_register_data
&7b29 8d 00 fe STA &fe00 ; video register number
&7b2c bd f5 7b LDA &7bf5,X ; video_register_data + 1
&7b2f 8d 01 fe STA &fe01 ; video register value
&7b32 e8       INX
&7b33 e8       INX
&7b34 e0 08    CPX #&08
&7b36 d0 ee    BNE &7b26 ; write_video_registers_loop
&7b38 a9 0d    LDA #&0d
&7b3a 8d 00 fe STA &fe00 ; video register number
&7b3d a9 00    LDA #&00
&7b3f 8d 01 fe STA &fe01 ; video register value
&7b42 a9 06    LDA #&06
&7b44 8d 00 fe STA &fe00 ; video register number
&7b47 a9 20    LDA #&20
&7b49 8d 01 fe STA &fe01 ; video register value
&7b4c a9 07    LDA #&07
&7b4e 8d 00 fe STA &fe00 ; video register number
&7b51 a9 22    LDA #&22
&7b53 8d 01 fe STA &fe01 ; video register value
&7b56 a9 00    LDA #&00
&7b58 a8       TAY
&7b59 85 70    STA &70 ; source_address_low
&7b5b 85 72    STA &72 ; target_address_low
&7b5d a9 04    LDA #&04
&7b5f 85 73    STA &73 ; target_address_high
&7b61 a9 6e    LDA #&6e
&7b63 85 71    STA &71 ; source_address_high
; move_6e00_to_7aff_loop                                            # Move &6e00 - &7aff to &0400 - &10ff
&7b65 b1 70    LDA (&70),Y ; source_address
&7b67 91 72    STA (&72),Y ; target_address
&7b69 c8       INY
&7b6a d0 f9    BNE &7b65 ; move_6e00_to_7aff_loop
&7b6c e6 71    INC &71 ; source_address_high
&7b6e e6 73    INC &73 ; target_address_high
&7b70 a5 73    LDA &73 ; target_address_high
&7b72 c9 11    CMP #&11
&7b74 d0 ef    BNE &7b65 ; move_6e00_to_7aff_loop
&7b76 a0 00    LDY #&00
&7b78 98       TYA
&7b79 85 70    STA &70 ; target_address_low
&7b7b 85 72    STA &72 ; source_address_low
&7b7d a9 58    LDA #&58
&7b7f 85 71    STA &71 ; target_address_high
&7b81 a9 65    LDA #&65
&7b83 85 73    STA &73 ; source_address_high
; move_6500_to_6cff_loop                                            # Move &6500 - &6cff to &5800 - &5fff
&7b85 b1 72    LDA (&72),Y ; source_address
&7b87 91 70    STA (&70),Y ; target_address
&7b89 c8       INY
&7b8a d0 f9    BNE &7b85 ; move_6500_to_6cff_loop
&7b8c e6 73    INC &73 ; source_address_high
&7b8e e6 71    INC &71 ; target_address_high
&7b90 a5 71    LDA &71 ; target_address_high
&7b92 c9 60    CMP #&60
&7b94 d0 ef    BNE &7b85 ; move_6500_to_6cff_loop
&7b96 a2 00    LDX #&00
; move_memory_loop
&7b98 bd 00 6d LDA &6d00,X                                          # Move &6d00 - &6d3f to &03a0 - &03df
&7b9b 9d a0 03 STA &03a0,X
&7b9e bd 40 6d LDA &6d40,X                                          # Move &6d40 - &6d7f to &0180 - &01bf
&7ba1 9d 80 01 STA &0180,X
&7ba4 e8       INX
&7ba5 e0 40    CPX #&40
&7ba7 d0 ef    BNE &7b98 ; move_memory_loop
&7ba9 a2 00    LDX #&00
&7bab a2 00    LDX #&00
; move_memory_two_loop
&7bad bd 80 6d LDA &6d80,X                                          # Move &6d80 - &6d9f to &0380 - &039f
&7bb0 9d 80 03 STA &0380,X
&7bb3 e8       INX
&7bb4 e0 20    CPX #&20
&7bb6 d0 f5    BNE &7bad ; move_memory_two_loop
&7bb8 a9 0b    LDA #&0b ; Set auto-repeat delay
&7bba a2 00    LDX #&00
&7bbc 20 f4 ff JSR &fff4 ; OSBYTE
&7bbf a2 00    LDX #&00
&7bc1 86 9f    STX &9f ; level
&7bc3 86 9e    STX &9e ; level_sprite_base
&7bc5 8a       TXA
; wipe_0100_to_017f_loop
&7bc6 9d 00 01 STA &0100,X                                          # Wipe &0100 - &017f
&7bc9 e8       INX
&7bca e0 80    CPX #&80
&7bcc d0 f8    BNE &7bc6 ; wipe_0100_to_017f_loop
&7bce a9 00    LDA #&00
&7bd0 8d 62 02 STA &0262 ; os_sound_suppression                     # Set to zero to permit sound
&7bd3 aa       TAX
; move_memory_three_loop
&7bd4 bd a0 6d LDA &6da0,X                                          # Move &6da0 - &6dff to &0300 - &035f
&7bd7 9d 00 03 STA &0300,X
&7bda e8       INX
&7bdb e0 60    CPX #&60
&7bdd d0 f5    BNE &7bd4 ; move_memory_three_loop
&7bdf a9 d4    LDA #&d4
&7be1 8d 62 fe STA &fe62 ; User VIA data direction register B
&7be4 a2 ff    LDX #&ff
&7be6 9a       TXS
&7be7 a9 08    LDA #&08
&7be9 8d 00 fe STA &fe00 ; video register number
&7bec a9 00    LDA #&00
&7bee 8d 01 fe STA &fe01 ; video register value
&7bf1 4c 00 11 JMP &1100 ; new_game

; video_register_data
&7bf4 01 20 ; R1: Number of characters per line
&7bf6 0a 20 ; R10: Cursor start register
&7bf8 02 2e ; R2: Horizontal sync position register
&7bfa 0c 0c ; R12: Displayed screen start address register (high)   # Screen starts at &6000

; unused 
&7bfc 6b 1d 05 00
